					<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content"><div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr"><div class="disambig-see-also"><i>See also:</i> <b class="Latn"><a href="/wiki/tak%C3%A9" title="také">také</a></b>, <b class="Latn"><a href="/wiki/T%C4%81ke" title="Tāke">Tāke</a></b><span class="serial-comma">,</span><span class="serial-and"> <i>and</i></span> <b class="Latn"><a href="/wiki/t%C3%A5ke" title="tåke">tåke</a></b></div>
<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" />
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="English">English</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: English"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Alternative_forms">Alternative forms</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/tek#English" title="tek">tek</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northumbrian_dialect" class="extiw" title="w:Northumbrian dialect">Northumbria</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_English" class="extiw" title="w:Middle English">Middle English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/taken#Middle_English" title="taken">taken</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take, lay hold of, grasp, strike</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/tacan#Old_English" title="tacan">tacan</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to grasp, touch</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, probably of <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_Germanic_languages" class="extiw" title="w:North Germanic languages">North Germanic</a></span> origin, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Norse" class="extiw" title="w:Old Norse">Old Norse</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="non"><a href="/wiki/taka#Old_Norse" title="taka">taka</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to touch, take</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Germanic language">Proto-Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gem-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/t%C4%93kan%C4%85" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/tēkaną">&#42;tēkaną</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to touch</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from pre-Germanic <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro">&#42;deh₁g-</i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to touch</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, possibly a phonetically altered form of <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/teh%E2%82%82g-#Proto-Indo-European:_touch" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/teh₂g-">&#42;te-th₂g-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to touch, take</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span> (see there for details).<sup id="cite&#95;ref-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-1"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-2"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Gradually displaced native <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_language" class="extiw" title="w:English language">English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/nim#English" title="nim">nim</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_English" class="extiw" title="w:Middle English">Middle English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/nimen#Middle_English" title="nimen">nimen</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/niman#Old_English" title="niman">niman</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
<div class="NavFrame">
<div class="NavHead" style="text-align: left;">Cognates</div>
<div class="NavContent boxcontent" style="text-align: left;">
<p>Cognate with <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scots_language" class="extiw" title="w:Scots language">Scots</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sco"><a href="/wiki/tak#Scots" title="tak">tak</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Icelandic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Icelandic language">Icelandic</a></span> and <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nynorsk" class="extiw" title="w:Nynorsk">Norwegian Nynorsk</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="nn"><a href="/wiki/taka#Norwegian_Nynorsk" title="taka">taka</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bokm%C3%A5l" class="extiw" title="w:Bokmål">Norwegian Bokmål</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="nb"><a href="/wiki/ta#Norwegian_Bokmål" title="ta">ta</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Swedish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Swedish language">Swedish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sv"><a href="/wiki/ta#Swedish" title="ta">ta</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Danish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Danish language">Danish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="da"><a href="/wiki/tage#Danish" title="tage">tage</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take, seize</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Frisian_language" class="extiw" title="w:West Frisian language">West Frisian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="fy"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#West_Frisian">take</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="fy"><a href="/wiki/taakje#West_Frisian" title="taakje">taakje</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to grab, steal</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dutch_language" class="extiw" title="w:Dutch language">Dutch</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="nl"><a href="/wiki/taken#Dutch" title="taken">taken</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take; grasp</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_Low_German" class="extiw" title="w:Middle Low German">Middle Low German</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gml"><a href="/wiki/tacken#Middle_Low_German" title="tacken">tacken</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to grasp</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. English <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/thack#English" title="thack">thack</a></i> may be from the same root. Compare <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/tackle#English" title="tackle">tackle</a></i>. Despite superficial similarity, unrelated to <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/tek-" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/tek-">&#42;tek-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to take by hand, obtain</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, which is instead cognate with English <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/thig#English" title="thig">thig</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to beg</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
</div></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Verb">Verb</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="en">take</strong> (<i>third-person singular simple present</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en s-verb-form-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/takes#English" title="takes">takes</a></b>, <i>present participle</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en ing-form-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/taking#English" title="taking">taking</a></b>, <i>simple past</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en spast-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/took#English" title="took">took</a></b>, <i>past participle</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en past&#124;part-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/taken#English" title="taken">taken</a></b> <i>or</i> <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><span class="usage-label-sense"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#archaic" title="Appendix:Glossary">archaic</a>&#32;or&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scottish_English" class="extiw" title="w:Scottish English">Scotland</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en past&#124;part-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/tane#English" title="tane">tane</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/get" title="get">get</a> into one's hands, <a href="/wiki/possession" title="possession">possession</a><span class="serial-comma">,</span> or <a href="/wiki/control" title="control">control</a>, with or without <a href="/wiki/force" title="force">force</a>.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/confiscate#English" title="confiscate">confiscate</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/seize#English" title="seize">seize</a></span>; <i>see also</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:take#English" title="Thesaurus:take">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">take</span></a></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">They <b>took</b> Charlton's gun from his cold, dead hands.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I'll <b>take</b> that plate off the table.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1627</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Hakewill" class="extiw" title="w:George Hakewill">G&#91;eorge&#93; H&#91;akewill&#93;</a>, <cite>An Apologie of the Power and Prouidence of God in the Gouernment of the World.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Or An Examination and Censure of the Common Errour Touching Natures Perpetuall and Vniuersall Decay, Diuided into Foure Bookes: Whereof the First Treates of This Pretended Decay in Generall, together with Some Preparatiues thereunto. The Second of the Pretended Decay of the Heauens and Elements, together with that of the Elementary Bodies, Man only Excepted. The Third of the Pretended Decay of Mankinde in Regard of Age and Duration, of Strength and Stature, of Arts and Wits. The Fourth of this Pretended Decay in Matter of Manners, together with a Large Proofe of the Future Consummation of the World from the Testimony of the Gentiles, and the Vses which We are to Draw from the Consideration thereof. By G. H. D.D.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, Oxford, Oxfordshire&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed by">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> Iohn Lichfield and William Turner,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="printers to the famous Vniuersity.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/863459859">→OCLC</a></small>, book IV, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/b30326783/page/402/mode/1up">pages <span class="None" lang="und">402–403</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">All theſe Ceremonies thus being performed&#59; the Prince which ſucceeded <b>taketh</b> a torch, and firſt putteth to the fire himſelfe, and after him all the reſt of the company, and by and by as the fire was kindled out of the toppe of the higheſt turret, an Eagle was let fly to carry vp his ſoule into heaven, and ſo he was afterward reputed, and by the <i>Romanes</i> adored among the reſt of the <i>Gods&#58;</i> <span class="q-hellip-sp"> </span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1637</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Heywood" class="extiw" title="w:Thomas Heywood">Tho&#91;mas&#93; Heywood</a>, <cite>Londini Speculum: Or, Londons Mirror,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Exprest in Sundry Triumphs, Pageants, and Showes, at the Initiation of the Right Honorable Richard Fenn, into the Mairolty of the Famous and Farre Renovvned City London. All the Charge and Expence of These Laborious Projects both by Water and Land, Being the Sole Undertaking of the Right Worshipful Company of the Habberdashers.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Imprinted at London by">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nicholas_Okes" class="extiw" title="w:Nicholas Okes">I<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>ohn<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Okes</a><span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="dwelling in Little St. Bartholmews.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1243827783">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/heywoodlondinispeculum/page/n7/mode/1up">signature B, verso</a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">That viſage miſ-becomes, thy Pipe &#47; Caſt from thee, Warlike dame, &#47; <b>Take</b> unto thee thy wonted Armes, &#47; And keepe thy Cheekes in frame.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1963</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Margery_Allingham" class="extiw" title="w:Margery Allingham">Margery Allingham</a>, “Meeting Point”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_China_Governess" class="extiw" title="w:The China Governess">The China Governess&#58; A Mystery</a></cite>, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chatto_%26_Windus" class="extiw" title="w:Chatto &amp; Windus">Chatto &amp; Windus</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/483591931">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/in.ernet.dli.2015.460907/page/n231/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">232</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">She <b>took</b> the policeman’s helmet and placed it on a chair, and unfolded his tunic to shake it and fold it up again for him.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1997</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Carlin" class="extiw" title="w:George Carlin">George Carlin</a>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brain_Droppings" class="extiw" title="w:Brain Droppings">Brain Droppings</a></cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hachette_Books" class="extiw" title="w:Hachette Books">Hyperion</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/0-786-86313-7" title="Special:BookSources/0-786-86313-7">→ISBN</a></small>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/36084460">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/braindroppings0000carl/page/67/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">67</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">We <b>take</b>, <b>take</b>, <b>take</b> until we can't take anymore. Maybe it's because our inner nature is not primarily one of giving, but of taking. Even these things we <b>take</b> that should balance our lives and give us rest do not. We make work out of them. We do them aggressively&#59; always in control. <b>Take</b>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/seize" title="seize">seize</a> or <a href="/wiki/capture" title="capture">capture</a>.

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1587–1588</b> (date written)</span>, &#91;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christopher_Marlowe" class="extiw" title="w:Christopher Marlowe">Christopher Marlowe</a>&#93;, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tamburlaine" class="extiw" title="w:Tamburlaine">Tamburlaine the Great. <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Who, from a Scythian Shepheard, by His Rare and Wonderfull Conquestes, Became a Most Puissant and Mightie Monarch: and (for His Tyrannie, and Terrour in Warre) was Tearmed, the Scourge of God. Now Newly Published.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> The First Part<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="of the Two Tragicall Discourses, as They were Sundrie Times Most Stately Shewed vpon Stages in the Citie of London. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/catch" title="catch">catch</a> or get possession of (fish or game).

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1839</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Darwin" class="extiw" title="w:Charles Darwin">Charles Darwin</a>, chapter XII, in <cite>Narrative of the Surveying Voyages of His Majesty’s Ships <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HMS_Aid_(1809)" class="extiw" title="w:HMS Aid (1809)">Adventure</a> and <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HMS_Beagle" class="extiw" title="w:HMS Beagle">Beagle</a>, between the Years 1826 and 1836,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Describing Their Examination of the Southern Shores of South America, and the Beagle’s Circumnavigation of the Globe. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/cricket" title="cricket">cricket</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/catch" title="catch">catch</a> the ball; <i>especially</i> as a <a href="/wiki/wicket-keeper" class="mw-redirect" title="wicket-keeper">wicket-keeper</a> and after the <a href="/wiki/batsman" title="batsman">batsman</a> has missed or edged it.</li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/appropriate" title="appropriate">appropriate</a> or <a href="/wiki/transfer" title="transfer">transfer</a> into one's own possession, sometimes by physically carrying off.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Billy <b>took</b> her pencil.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/exact" title="exact">exact</a>.

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1849</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Herman_Melville" class="extiw" title="w:Herman Melville">Herman Melville</a>, chapter XXII, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mardi" class="extiw" title="w:Mardi">Mardi: And a Voyage Thither.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="&#91;...&#93; In Two Volumes.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a></cite>, volume I, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harper_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:Harper (publisher)">Harper &amp; Brothers</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="publishers, 82 Cliff Street.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/2413019">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/mardiandvoyageth01melvrich/page/92/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">92</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">"Load away now, and <b>take</b> thy revenge, my fine fellow," said Samoa to himself. <li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1913</b></span> November, Rabindranath Tagore, “The Problem of Evil”, in <cite>Sādhanā: The Realisation of Life</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Macmillan_Inc." class="extiw" title="w:Macmillan Inc.">The Macmillan Company</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1114470">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/sadhanarealisati00tago/page/65/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">65</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">It is only when we invoke the aid of pain for our self-gratification that she becomes evil and <b>takes</b> her vengeance for the insult done to her by hurling us into misery.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/capture" title="capture">capture</a> or <a href="/wiki/win" title="win">win</a> (a <a href="/wiki/piece" title="piece">piece</a> or <a href="/wiki/trick" title="trick">trick</a>) in a <a href="/wiki/game" title="game">game</a>.

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/receive" title="receive">receive</a> or <a href="/wiki/accept" title="accept">accept</a> (something, especially something which was <a href="/wiki/given" title="given">given</a>).
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/garner#English" title="garner">garner</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/get#English" title="get">get</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/obtain#English" title="obtain">obtain</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/win#English" title="win">win</a></span>; <i>see also</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:receive#English" title="Thesaurus:receive">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">receive</span></a></span></dd>
<dd><span class="nyms antonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Antonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/give#English" title="give">give</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>took</b> third place</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>took</b> bribes</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The camera <b>takes</b> 35mm film.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1611</b></span>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bible" class="extiw" title="w:Bible">The Holy Bible,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Conteyning the Old Testament, and the New. <li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>1980</b> March 7, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Billy_Joel" class="extiw" title="w:Billy Joel">Billy Joel</a>, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/You_May_Be_Right" class="extiw" title="w:You May Be Right">You May Be Right</a>”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glass_Houses_(album)" class="extiw" title="w:Glass Houses (album)">Glass Houses</a></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ilsv0C1-aBw">[2]</a></sup>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">You were lonely for a man<br />I said, "<b>Take</b> me as I am"<br />'Cause you might enjoy some madness for a while</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/receive" title="receive">receive</a> or <a href="/wiki/accept" title="accept">accept</a> (something) as <a href="/wiki/payment" title="payment">payment</a> or <a href="/wiki/compensation" title="compensation">compensation</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The store doesn't <b>take</b> checks.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">She wouldn't <b>take</b> any money for her help.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Do you <b>take</b> plastic&#63;</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The vending machine only <b>takes</b> bills, it doesn't <b>take</b> coins.</i></div></dd></dl>

<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1860</b></span> August–December, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Ruskin" class="extiw" title="w:John Ruskin">John Ruskin</a>, “Essay I. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/accept" title="accept">accept</a> and <a href="/wiki/follow" title="follow">follow</a> (advice<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>take</b> my advice</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#a." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="ante">a.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1705</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Locke" class="extiw" title="w:John Locke">John Locke</a>, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Of_the_Conduct_of_the_Understanding" class="extiw" title="w:Of the Conduct of the Understanding">Of the Conduct of the Understanding</a>”, in <cite>Posthumous Works of Mr. John Locke:<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Viz. I. Of the Conduct of the Understanding. II. An Examination of P&#91;ère&#93; Malebranche’s Opinion of Seeing All Things in God. III. A Discourse of Miracles. IV. Part of a Fourth Letter for Toleration. V. Memoirs Relating to the Life of Anthony First Earl of Shaftesbury. To which is Added, VI. His New Method of a Common-place-book, Written Originally in French, and Now Translated into English.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed by W. B. for">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Awnsham_Churchill" class="extiw" title="w:Awnsham Churchill">A<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>wnsham<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></a> and J<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>ohn<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Churchill,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="at the Black Swan in Pater-Noster-Row.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1706</span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/6963663">→OCLC</a></small>, <span class="None" lang="und">§&#160;13</span>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://books.google.com/books?id=o0EVAAAAQAAJ&amp;pg=PA49">page <span class="None" lang="und">49</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Between theſe, thoſe ſeem to do beſt who <b>taking</b> material and uſeful hints, ſometimes from ſingle matters of Fact, carry them in their Minds to be judg'd of, by what they ſhall find in Hiſtory to confirm or reverſe theſe imperfect Obſervations&#59; which may be eſtabliſh'd into Rules fit to be rely'd on, when they are juſtify'd by a ſufficient and wary Induction of Particulars.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/receive" title="receive">receive</a> into some relationship.
<dl><dd><span class="h-usage-example collocation"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>take</b> a wife</i></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The school only <b>takes</b> new students in the fall.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The therapist wouldn't <b>take</b> him as a client.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/law#English" title="law">law</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/receive" title="receive">receive</a> or <a href="/wiki/acquire" title="acquire">acquire</a> (property) by law (e.g. as an heir).
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1831</b></span> June, J. Duncan, “Lodge <i>against</i> Simonton”, in <cite>Reports of Cases Argued and Adjudged in the Supreme Court of Pennsylvania</cite>, third edition, volume II, Philadelphia, P.A.&#58; Kay &amp; Brother, published <span class="None" lang="und">1880</span>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/reportsofcasesar02rawliala/page/442/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">442</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">There was no <a href="/wiki/intestacy#Noun" title="intestacy">intestacy</a>, and they did not <b>take</b> under the will as heirs, but the widow and the children, under the residuary devise, take as tenants in common.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/accept" title="accept">accept</a>, be <a href="/wiki/give" title="give">given</a> (rightly or wrongly), or <a href="/wiki/assume" title="assume">assume</a> (especially as if by <a href="/wiki/right" title="right">right</a>).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>took</b> all the credit for the project, although he had done almost none of the work.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">She <b>took</b> the blame, in the public's eyes, although the debacle was more her husband's fault than her own.</i></div></dd></dl></li></ol></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/remove" title="remove">remove</a>.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/knock_off#English" title="knock off">knock off</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/subduct#English" title="subduct">subduct</a></span>; <i>see also</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:remove#English" title="Thesaurus:remove">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">remove</span></a></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>take</b> two eggs from the carton</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#a." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="ante">a.</abbr></a></i> <b>1717</b> (date written), <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_South" class="extiw" title="w:Robert South">Robert South</a>, “Sermon VI”, in <cite>Five Additional Volumes of Sermons Preached upon Several Occasions.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="&#91;...&#93; Now First Printed from the Author’s Manuscripts. <li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1845</b></span> February, — Quarles &#91;pseudonym&#59; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edgar_Allan_Poe" class="extiw" title="w:Edgar Allan Poe">Edgar Allan Poe</a>&#93;, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Raven" class="extiw" title="w:The Raven">The Raven</a>”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_American_Review:_A_Whig_Journal" class="extiw" title="w:The American Review: A Whig Journal">The American Review</a></cite>, volume I, number II, New York, N.Y.; London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wiley_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:Wiley (publisher)">Wiley &amp; Putnam</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="161, Broadway, and 6, Waterloo Place, Regent-Street, London. <ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/remove" title="remove">remove</a> or <a href="/wiki/end" title="end">end</a> by <a href="/wiki/death" title="death">death</a>; to <a href="/wiki/kill" title="kill">kill</a>.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/do_in#English" title="do in">do in</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/terminate#English" title="terminate">terminate</a></span>; <i>see also</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:kill#English" title="Thesaurus:kill">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">kill</span></a></span></dd>

<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/have#English" title="have">have</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/sleep_with#English" title="sleep with">sleep with</a></span>; <i>see also</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:copulate_with#English" title="Thesaurus:copulate with">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">copulate with</span></a></span></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1990</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pat_Booth" class="extiw" title="w:Pat Booth">Pat Booth</a>, <cite>Malibu</cite>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Crown_Publishers,_Inc." class="extiw" title="w:Crown Publishers, Inc.">Crown Publishers, Inc.</a>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/isbn_0000017507/page/222/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">222</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Sometimes he would have her standing up by the side of the bed, not bothering to undress, merely undoing his fly and using her like a cheap envelope to receive his lust. At others he would <b>take</b> her on the floor of her clothes closet and then leave her, locked in for the rest of the night, awash with his sex, until her embarrassed maid freed her the next morning.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>2002</b> September 16, INCESTOR, “STORY: "Horny Peeping Sister" (6/9) (mf, voy, family) 566710”, in <cite><kbd>alt.sex.stories</kbd></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://groups.google.com/g/alt.sex.stories/c/dt5nwrxL8wA/m/AzIwhXhR1GQJ">[3]</a></sup> (<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Usenet" class="extiw" title="w:Usenet">Usenet</a>):</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I wonder what it would feel like to <b>take</b> two cocks at the same time.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1967</b></span> &#91;<span class="None" lang="und">1945</span>&#93;, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Georges_Simenon" class="extiw" title="w:Georges Simenon">Georges Simenon</a>, translated by Jean Stewart, <cite>Monsieur Monde Vanishes</cite>, New York, N.Y.; London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harcourt_Brace_Jovanovich" class="extiw" title="w:Harcourt Brace Jovanovich">Harcourt Brace Jovanovich</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/0-151-62098-9" title="Special:BookSources/0-151-62098-9">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/monsieurmondevan00sime_0/page/126/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">126</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">He remembered her look of distress, her childish "Oh&#33;" when he <b>took</b> her for the first time, clumsily, because he felt ashamed. And each time after that, each time they had sex together, though he tried to be as gentle as possible, he knew she was wearing the same expression, he avoided seeing her face, and thus it happened that instead of being a pleasure the sexual act became an ordeal.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2012</b></span>, <cite>The Onion Book of Known Knowledge: A Definitive Encyclopaedia of Existing Information: In 27 Excruciating Volumes</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Little,_Brown_and_Company" class="extiw" title="w:Little, Brown and Company">Little, Brown and Company</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-316-13326-5" title="Special:BookSources/978-0-316-13326-5">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/onionbookofknown0000unse/page/8/mode/1up">page 8</a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Modern Amsterdam is among Europe's most progressive cities, leading in such fields as design, fisting, felching, civil engineering, fashion, five-ways, pony play, computer science, and transportation. Its stock exchange is the oldest in Europe, and lovely Anastasia <b>takes</b> six men at once while shitting into a crystal goblet during her live show on the Bloedstraat at 11&#58;30 p.m. every Tuesday.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>2014</b> July 3, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mock_the_Week" class="extiw" title="w:Mock the Week">Mock the Week</a></cite>, season <span class="None" lang="und">13</span>, episode 4, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Susan_Calman" class="extiw" title="w:Susan Calman">Susan Calman</a> (actor):</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">And the queen <b>takes</b> the bishop...this is turning out to be quite the royal wedding&#33; &#91;winks at the camera&#93;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/defeat" title="defeat">defeat</a> (someone or something) in a <a href="/wiki/fight" title="fight">fight</a>.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/beat#English" title="beat">beat</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Don't try to <b>take</b> that guy. He's bigger than you.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The woman guarding us looks like a professional, but I can <b>take</b> her&#33;</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>1840</b> April – <b>1841</b> November, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Dickens" class="extiw" title="w:Charles Dickens">Charles Dickens</a>, “Chapter the Sixth”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Old_Curiosity_Shop" class="extiw" title="w:The Old Curiosity Shop">The Old Curiosity Shop. A Tale.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="&#91;...&#93; With Illustrations by George Cattermole and Hablot K. Browne. Complete in One Volume.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a></cite>, volume I, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chapman_%26_Hall" class="extiw" title="w:Chapman &amp; Hall">Chapman and Hall</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Strand.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1841</span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1109979921">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/oldcuriosityshop1841dick/page/n50/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">111</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">"I'll stop 'em'" cried Quilp, diving into the little counting-house and returning with a thick stick, "I'll stop 'em. Now my boys fight away. I'll fight you both, I'll <b>take</b> both of you, both together, both together&#33;"</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1878</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Black_(novelist)" class="extiw" title="w:William Black (novelist)">William Black</a>, “Fionaghal”, in <cite>Macleod of Dare.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="A Novel. &#91;...&#93; In Three Volumes.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, volume I, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Macmillan_Publishers" class="extiw" title="w:Macmillan Publishers">Macmillan and Co.</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1016665906">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/macleodofdarenov01blac/page/57/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">57</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">"What is cruel now was not cruel then," he said&#59; "it was a way of fighting&#59; it was what is called an ambush now—enticing your enemy, and then <b>taking</b> him at a disadvantage. And if you did not do that to him he would do it to you. And when a man is mad with anger or revenge, what does he care for anything&#63;"</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/grasp" title="grasp">grasp</a> or <a href="/wiki/grip" title="grip">grip</a>.

<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed for">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_Johnson_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:Joseph Johnson (publisher)">J<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>oseph<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Johnson</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="in St. Paul’s Church-yard, and entered at the Stationers’ Hall.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1055941330">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/arabiantalefromu00beck/page/119/mode/1up">pages <span class="None" lang="und">119–120</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">The young females ſeeing him approach in ſuch haste&#59; <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/select" title="select">select</a> or <a href="/wiki/choose" title="choose">choose</a>; to <a href="/wiki/pick" title="pick">pick</a>.

<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I'll <b>take</b> two sugars in my coffee, please.</i></div></dd></dl>

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1882</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bret_Harte" class="extiw" title="w:Bret Harte">Bret Harte</a>, “<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span><i>Found at Blazing Star</i><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span>”, in <cite>Flip; and Found at Blazing Star</cite>, Boston, Mass.; New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Houghton_Mifflin_Harcourt" class="extiw" title="w:Houghton Mifflin Harcourt">Houghton, Mifflin and Company</a><span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="New York: 11 East Seventeenth Street; The Riverside Press, Cambridge &#91;Mass.&#93;">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1525509">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/flipandfoundatbl00hartrich/page/117/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">117</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Heeding the wise caution of his com rades, he <b>took</b> the habit of wearing the ring only at night. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/carry" title="carry">carry</a> or <a href="/wiki/lead" title="lead">lead</a> (something or someone).

The part they <b>take</b> againſt me is from zeal to the cauſe. It is well&#33; It is perfectly well&#33; I have to do homage to their juſtice.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;especially of a vehicle</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/transport" title="transport">transport</a> or <a href="/wiki/carry" title="carry">carry</a>; to convey to another place.

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1925</b></span>, Aldous Huxley, <cite>Along the Road: Notes and Essays of a Tourist</cite>, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chatto_%26_Windus" class="extiw" title="w:Chatto &amp; Windus">Chatto &amp; Windus</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/806315003">→OCLC</a></small>, part I (Travel in General), <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/in.ernet.dli.2015.459146/page/n19/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">16</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">All I claim for the ten-horse-power Citroën is this&#58; that it works. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;of a path, road<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/lead" title="lead">lead</a> (to a place); to serve as a means of reaching.

<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Stone Street <b>took</b> us right past the store.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1884</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_Anthony_Froude" class="extiw" title="w:James Anthony Froude">James Anthony Froude</a>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Carlyle" class="extiw" title="w:Thomas Carlyle">Thomas Carlyle</a>: A History of His Life in London, 1834–1881<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="In Two Volumes">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, volume I, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Longman" class="extiw" title="w:Longman">Longmans, Green, and Co.</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1131168422">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/thomascarlyleah11frougoog/page/188/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">188</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Our old wooden Battersea bridge <b>takes</b> me over the river&#59; in ten minutes' swift trotting I am fairly away from the monster and its bricks.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/pass" title="pass">pass</a> (or attempt to pass) through or around.

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1796</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samuel_Taylor_Coleridge" class="extiw" title="w:Samuel Taylor Coleridge">S&#91;amuel&#93; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed for">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Robinson_(bookseller)" class="extiw" title="w:George Robinson (bookseller)">G<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>eorge<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> G<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>eorge<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> and J<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>ohn<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Robinsons</a>, and <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_Cottle" class="extiw" title="w:Joseph Cottle">J<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>oseph<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Cottle</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="bookseller, Bristol.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/221351092">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/poemsonvarioussu00cole/page/93/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">93</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">And fain would <b>take</b> thee with me, in the Dell &#47; I <b>took</b> the old man aside, and two or three glasses of Old Crow launched him into reminiscence.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>1937</b> September 21, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/J._R._R._Tolkien" class="extiw" title="w:J. R. R. And with that he went off along the hedge <b>taking</b> the frightened hobbit with him.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#reflexive" title="Appendix:Glossary">reflexive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/go" title="go">go</a>.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2007</b></span>, Edwin B. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/use" title="use">use</a> as a means of <a href="/wiki/transportation" title="transportation">transportation</a>.

<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>took</b> the bus to London, and then <b>took</b> a train to Manchester.</i></div></dd>

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1892</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walter_Besant" class="extiw" title="w:Walter Besant">Walter Besant</a>, chapter III, in <cite>The Ivory Gate<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="A Novel">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harper_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:Harper (publisher)">Harper &amp; Brothers</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Franklin Square">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/16832619">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/ivorygatenovel00besa/page/48/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">48</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">To such men as Mr. Hellyer, who every night <b>take</b> much strong drink, and on no occasion whatever take any exercise, sixty is the grand climacteric. So I started to back away again into the bushes. But I hadn't backed more'n a couple of yards when I see something so amazing that I couldn't help scooching down behind the bayberries and looking at it.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/submit" title="submit">submit</a> to; to <a href="/wiki/endure" title="endure">endure</a> (without <a href="/wiki/ill_humor" title="ill humor">ill humor</a>, <a href="/wiki/resentment" title="resentment">resentment</a>, or physical <a href="/wiki/failure" title="failure">failure</a>).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>took</b> a pay cut</i></div></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example collocation"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>take</b> a joke</i></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">If you're in an abusive relationship, don't just sit and <b>take</b> it&#59; you can get help.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The hull <b>took</b> a lot of punishment before it broke.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I can <b>take</b> the noise, but I can't <b>take</b> the smell.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">That truck bed will only <b>take</b> two tons.</i></div></dd></dl>

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1552</b></span> (date written), <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nicholas_Udall" class="extiw" title="w:Nicholas Udall">Nicholas Udall</a>, <cite><span style="font-style:normal">[</span><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ralph_Roister_Doister" class="extiw" title="w:Ralph Roister Doister">Ralph Roister Doister</a><span style="font-style:normal">]</span></cite>, <span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>London<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span>&#58; <span class="None" lang="und">&#91;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#s.n." title="Appendix:Glossary">s.n.</a>&#93;</span>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1566?</span>&#59; republished as <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edward_Arber" class="extiw" title="w:Edward Arber">Edward Arber</a>, editor, <cite>Roister Doister.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Written, Probably also Represented, before 1553. <li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1950</b></span>, E<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>wdin<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Basil Redlich, <cite>The Early Traditions of Genesis</cite>, London&#58; Gerald Duckworth &amp; Co. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/draw" title="draw">draw</a>, <a href="/wiki/derive" title="derive">derive</a>, or <a href="/wiki/deduce" title="deduce">deduce</a> (a meaning from something).

becauſe it is <b>taken</b> from the conſideration of the greateſt and moſt laſting happineſs and miſery that Humane nature is capable of.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/derive" title="derive">derive</a> (as a title); to <a href="/wiki/obtain" title="obtain">obtain</a> from a <a href="/wiki/source" title="source">source</a>.

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/catch" title="catch">catch</a> or <a href="/wiki/contract" title="contract">contract</a> (an illness<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.).

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/captivate" title="captivate">captivate</a> or <a href="/wiki/charm" title="charm">charm</a>; to gain or secure the interest or affection of.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>took</b> her fancy</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>took</b> her attention</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1839</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thomas_Moore" class="extiw" title="w:Thomas Moore">Thomas Moore</a>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Epicurean" class="extiw" title="w:The Epicurean">The Epicurean&#58; A Tale</a></cite>, London&#58; John Macrone, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/epicureantale00moorrich/page/n53/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">33</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I know not why, but there was a something in those half-seen features,—a charm in the very shadow that hung over their imagined beauty,—which <b>took</b> my fancy more than all the out-shining loveliness of her companions.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;of a material</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/absorb" title="absorb">absorb</a> or be <a href="/wiki/impregnate" title="impregnate">impregnated</a> by (dye, ink<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.); to be susceptible to being treated by (polish<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">cloth that <b>takes</b> dye well</i></div></dd>

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1972</b></span>, Anne Sinai, <cite>Israel &amp; the Arabs: Prelude to the Jewish State</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Facts_on_File,_Inc." class="extiw" title="w:Facts on File, Inc.">Facts on File, Inc.</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/0-871-96169-5" title="Special:BookSources/0-871-96169-5">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/israelarabsprelu0000sina_c3m4/page/107/mode/1up">pages <span class="None" lang="und">107–108</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">The British brought the ship into Haifa harbor. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/require" title="require">require</a> <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content">a person, resource or thing in order to achieve an outcome</span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span>.

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/proceed" title="proceed">proceed</a> to fill.

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/fill" title="fill">fill</a>, <a href="/wiki/occupy" title="occupy">occupy</a>, require, or use up (space).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">His collection <b>takes</b> a lot of space.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To fill or require: to <a href="/wiki/last" title="last">last</a> or <a href="/wiki/expend" title="expend">expend</a> (an amount of time).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Unloading the moving truck <b>took</b> us half a day, but this mess of a house will <b>take</b> us all weekend to tidy up. It <b>takes</b> ages to finish house moving&#33;</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Hunting that whale <b>takes</b> most of his free time.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The trip will <b>take</b> about ten minutes.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">It <b>takes</b> a while to get used to the smell.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1940</b></span>, Zane Grey, chapter 12, in <cite>30,000 on the Hoof</cite>, Roslyn, N.Y.&#58; Walter J. Black, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1909404">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/in.ernet.dli.2015.268038/page/n198/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">193</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">"Barbara, what I have to confess will amaze and grieve you," began Lucinda, with grave tenderness. "But it is best for your happiness, for the future that I see can be yours. And surely best for all of us Huetts. It has <b>taken</b> me years—years to come to this decision—to break one aspect of our happy home life here for a possible fuller and better one."</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/avail" title="avail">avail</a> oneself of; to <a href="/wiki/exploit" title="exploit">exploit</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>took</b> that opportunity to leave France.</i></div></dd></dl>

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>1726</b> October 28, &#91;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jonathan_Swift" class="extiw" title="w:Jonathan Swift">Jonathan Swift</a>&#93;, “The Humours and Dispositions of the <i>Laputians</i> Described.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="An Account of Their Learning.
