An Account of the Women.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>”, in <cite>Travels into Several Remote Nations of the World. <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="In Four Parts. By Lemuel Gulliver, First a Surgeon, and then a Captain of Several Ships">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <span style="font-style:normal">[</span><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulliver%27s_Travels" class="extiw" title="w:Gulliver&#39;s Travels">Gulliver’s Travels</a><span style="font-style:normal">]</span></cite>, volume II, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed for">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Benjamin_Motte" class="extiw" title="w:Benjamin Motte">Benj<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>amin<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Motte</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="at the Middle Temple-Gate in Fleet-street">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/995220039">→OCLC</a></small>, part III (A Voyage to Laputa, Balnibarbi, Glubbdubdribb, Luggnagg, and Japan), <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/travelsintosever02swif/page/23/mode/1up">pages <span class="None" lang="und">23–24</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">He firſt <b>took</b> my Altitude by a Quadrant, and then with Rule and Compaſſes, deſcribed the Dimenſions and Out-lines of my whole Body, all which he enter'd upon Paper, and in ſix days brought my Clothes very ill made, and quite out of ſhape, by happening to miſtake a Figure in the Calculation.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To write down; to get in, or as if in, writing.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>took</b> a mental inventory of his supplies.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">She <b>took</b> careful notes.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1924</b></span> <span class="None" lang="und">May 24 – July 12</span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/P._G._Wodehouse" class="extiw" title="w:P. G. Wodehouse">P&#91;elham&#93; G&#91;renville&#93; Wodehouse</a>, “A Marriage Has Been Arranged”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bill_the_Conqueror" class="extiw" title="w:Bill the Conqueror">Bill the Conqueror&#58; His Invasion of England in the Springtime</a></cite>, 10th edition, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Methuen_Publishing" class="extiw" title="w:Methuen Publishing">Methuen &amp; Co.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Ltd. 36 Essex Street W.C. London">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1931</span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1098883">→OCLC</a></small>, <span class="None" lang="und">§&#160;2</span>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/dli.ministry.10317/page/6/mode/1up">page 6</a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">The days when he was plain George Pyke, humble clerk in a solicitor’s office, and used to thrill at the soft voice of Lucy Maynard as she <b>took</b> the order for his frugal lunch at the Holborn Viaduct Cabin, had long since faded from his memory.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/make" title="make">make</a> (a photograph, film, or other <a href="/wiki/reproduction" title="reproduction">reproduction</a> of something).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">She <b>took</b> a video of their encounter.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Could you <b>take</b> a picture of us&#63;</i></div></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example collocation"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The police <b>took</b> his fingerprints.</i></span></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#dated" title="Appendix:Glossary">dated</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To make a picture, photograph<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc. of (a person, scene<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The photographer will <b>take</b> you sitting down.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">to <b>take</b> a group&#47;scene</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/obtain" title="obtain">obtain</a> <a href="/wiki/money" title="money">money</a> from, especially by <a href="/wiki/swindling" title="swindling">swindling</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>took</b> me for ten grand</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;now chiefly by enrolling in a class or course</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/apply" title="apply">apply</a> oneself to the <a href="/wiki/study" title="study">study</a> of.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">As a child, she <b>took</b> ballet.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Next semester, I plan to <b>take</b> math, physics, literature, and art history.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To deal with.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>take</b> matters as they arise</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/consider" title="consider">consider</a> in a particular way, or to consider as an example.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I've had a lot of problems recently&#58; <b>take</b> last Monday, for example. My car broke down on the way to work. Then <span class="q-hellip-sp"> </span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"> </span> etc.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/baseball" title="baseball">baseball</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/decline" title="decline">decline</a> to <a href="/wiki/swing" title="swing">swing</a> at (a pitched ball); to <a href="/wiki/refrain" title="refrain">refrain</a> from hitting at, and allow to pass.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He'll probably <b>take</b> this one.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To accept as an input to a relation.
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/grammar" title="grammar">grammar</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To have to be used with (a certain grammatical form<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">This verb <b>takes</b> the dative&#59; that verb <b>takes</b> the genitive.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/mathematics" title="mathematics">mathematics</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/computing#Noun" title="computing">computing</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To accept (zero or more <a href="/wiki/argument" title="argument">arguments</a>).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The function <b>takes</b> two arguments, an array of size <i>n</i> and an integer <i>k</i>.</i></div></dd></dl></li></ol></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;Greece<span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;Cyprus<span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#informal" title="Appendix:Glossary">informal</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/buy" title="buy">buy</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>take</b> a ticket</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To get or <a href="/wiki/accept" title="accept">accept</a> (something) into one's <a href="/wiki/possession" title="possession">possession</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">My husband and I have a dysfunctional marriage. He just <b>takes</b> and <b>takes</b>&#59; he never gives.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To engage, take hold or have effect.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1631</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Francis_Bacon" class="extiw" title="w:Francis Bacon">Francis &#91;Bacon&#93;</a>, “II. So, Great <i>Flames</i>, whiles the one implelleth the other, giue a bellowing <i>Sound</i>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">of ink, dye<span class="serial-comma">,</span> etc.</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/adhere" title="adhere">adhere</a> or be absorbed properly.

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2009</b></span>, Sheldon Russell, <cite>The Yard Dog</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minotaur_Books" class="extiw" title="w:Minotaur Books">Minotaur Books</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-312-56670-8" title="Special:BookSources/978-0-312-56670-8">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/yarddog00russ/page/210/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">210</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">At the depot, Hook climbed out, slamming the door twice before the latch <b>took</b>. <li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">possibly&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#dated" title="Appendix:Glossary">dated</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To win acceptance, favor or favorable reception; to charm people.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>1716</b> March 21 (first performance; Gregorian calendar), &#91;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_Addison" class="extiw" title="w:Joseph Addison">Joseph Addison</a>&#93;, “<a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_the-drummer-or-the-hau_addison-joseph_1715/page/n4/mode/1up">Prologue. A Comedy.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="As It is Acted at the Theatre-Royal in Drury-Lane, by His Majesty’s Servants.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a></cite>, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed for">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jacob_Tonson" class="extiw" title="w:Jacob Tonson">Jacob Tonson</a><span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="at Shakespear’s-Head, over-against Katharine-Street in the Strand.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1716 (indicated as 1715)</span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/722996968">→OCLC</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Each VVit may praiſe it, for his ovvn dear Sake, &#47; And hint He vvrit it, if the Thing ſhou'd <b>take</b>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1967</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Richard_Martin_Stern" class="extiw" title="w:Richard Martin Stern">Richard Martin Stern</a>, <cite>The Kessler Legacy</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charles_Scribner%27s_Sons" class="extiw" title="w:Charles Scribner&#39;s Sons">Charles Scribner's Sons</a>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/kesslerlegacy00ster/page/103/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">103</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Here was only cruelty and pain&#59; where was the loving side of Christianity&#63; "When I was young," I said, "I was vaccinated with religion, but the vaccination didn't <b>take</b>."</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#copulative" title="Appendix:Glossary">copulative</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To become; to be affected in a specified way.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">They <b>took</b> ill within 3 hours.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">She <b>took</b> sick with the flu.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;possibly&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#dated" title="Appendix:Glossary">dated</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To be able to be accurately or beautifully photographed.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1881</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jessie_Fothergill" class="extiw" title="w:Jessie Fothergill">Jessie Fothergill</a>, chapter IX, in <cite>Kith and Kin: A Novel</cite>, volume II, London&#58; Robert Bentley and Son, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/kithandkinanove01fothgoog/page/n268/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">259</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">'Photographs never do give anything but a pale imitation, you know, but the likenesses, as likenesses, are good. She "<b>takes</b> well" as they say, and those were done lately.'</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#dialectal" title="Appendix:Glossary">dialectal</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#proscribed" title="Appendix:Glossary">proscribed</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span><sup id="cite&#95;ref-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-3"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <span class="use-with-mention">An intensifier.</span>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1843</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harriet_Beecher_Stowe" class="extiw" title="w:Harriet Beecher Stowe">Harriet Beecher Stowe</a>, <cite>The Mayflower; Or, Sketches of Scenes and Characters Among the Descendants of the Pilgrims</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harper_%26_Brothers" class="extiw" title="w:Harper &amp; Brothers">Harper &amp; Brothers</a>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/mayflowerorsketc00stowiala/page/34/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">34</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I don't know but she would, but just then poor Sukey came in, and looked so frightened and scarey—Sukey is a pretty gal, and looks so trembling and delicate, that it's kinder a shame to plague her, and so I <b>took</b> and come away for that time.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1859</b></span>, George Meredith, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Ordeal_of_Richard_Feverel" class="extiw" title="w:The Ordeal of Richard Feverel">The Ordeal of Richard Feverel. A History of Father and Son.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="&#91;...&#93; In Three Volumes.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a></cite>, volume I, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chapman_%26_Hall" class="extiw" title="w:Chapman &amp; Hall">Chapman and Hall</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/213819910">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/ordealofrichardf01mere/page/100/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">100</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Speed-the-Plough lurched round on his elbow and regarded him indifferently. "Moighty foin, that be&#33; D'ye call that Doctrin'&#63; He bean't al'ays, or I shoon't be scrapin' my heels wi' nothin' to do, and what's warse, nothin' to eat. Why, look heer. Luck 's luck, and bad luck's the con-trary. Varmer Bollop, t'other day, has's rick burnt down. Next night his gran'ry's burnt. What do he <b>tak</b><span>'</span> and go and do&#63; He <b>takes</b>, and goes, and hangs unsel', and turns us out o' 'ploy. God warn't above the Devil then, I thinks, or I can't make out the reckonin'."</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1875</b></span>, Arthur Sketchley, <cite>Mrs. Brown at the Crystal Palace</cite>, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Routledge_and_Sons" class="extiw" title="w:George Routledge and Sons">George Routledge and Sons</a>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/22633663.78967.emory.edu/page/n104/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">100</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">As made Queen 'Lizzybeth swear like blazes, and ketched poor old Dizzy sich a smack o' the face, as sent 'im up in a corner a-wimperin' with 'is 'ankercher to 'is nose, as made Gladstin give a grin, tho' he took good care to keep out of old Betsey's way, as glared at 'im&#59; and then <b>took</b> and turned on me and says, "Let me give you a turn, for you're a-layin' on your back too much."</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1943</b></span>, Max Brand &#91;pseudonym&#59; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frederick_Schiller_Faust" class="extiw" title="w:Frederick Schiller Faust">Frederick Schiller Faust</a>&#93;, <cite>Silvertip's Trap</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dodd,_Mead_%26_Company" class="extiw" title="w:Dodd, Mead &amp; Company">Dodd, Mead &amp; Company</a>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/pg000044_202207/pg000024.jpg">page <span class="None" lang="und">30</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I took and beat the devil out of him. I got him against the wall, and the back of his head bumped the wall just when my fist hit his chin, and he went out like a light, and that's how he come to have that big cut on his chin, like you was talking about.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1985</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Darcy_O%27Brien" class="extiw" title="w:Darcy O&#39;Brien">Darcy O'Brien</a>, <cite>Two of a Kind: The Hillside Stranglers</cite>, New York, N.Y.; Scarborough, Ont.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_American_Library" class="extiw" title="w:New American Library">New American Library</a>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/twoofkindhillsid0000darc/page/34/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">34</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en"><span class="q-hellip-sp"></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"> </span> I went and kicked the door in and took care of some other people. Then I <b>took</b> and went back to the hotel—" ¶ "The hotel where you live, right&#63; The Gilbert Hotel&#63;" ¶ "Right. I <b>took</b> and went back to the hotel, took a shower, went out and talked to a police officer—" ¶ "A police officer. Sheriff's deputy&#63; LAPD&#63; What's his name&#63;" ¶ "Can't recall. Jim. Charlie, could be."</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/deliver" title="deliver">deliver</a>, <a href="/wiki/bring" title="bring">bring</a>, <a href="/wiki/give" title="give">give</a> (something) to (someone).
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1526</b></span>, &#91;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Tyndale" class="extiw" title="w:William Tyndale">William Tyndale</a>, transl.&#93;, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Testament" class="extiw" title="w:New Testament">The Newe Testamẽt<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="as it was Written and Caused to be Writtẽ by Them which Herde Yt. To whom also Oure Saveoure Christ Jesus Commaunded that They Shulde Preache it vnto Al Creatures.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a></cite> (<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tyndale_Bible" class="extiw" title="w:Tyndale Bible">Tyndale Bible</a>), [Worms, Germany&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Sch%C3%B6ffer" class="extiw" title="w:Peter Schöffer">Peter Schöffer</a>&#93;, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/762018299">→OCLC</a></small>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gospel_of_Matthew" class="extiw" title="w:Gospel of Matthew">Matthew</a> xxij&#58;&#91;19&#93;, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/1526-tyndale-nt/page/n33/mode/1up">folio xxxi, verso</a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Jeſus perceaved there wylynes ãd ſayde&#58; Why tempte ye me ye ypocrytes&#58; lett me ſe the tribute money. And they <b>toke</b> hym a peny.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a>&#32;outside&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#dialectal" title="Appendix:Glossary">dialects</a>&#32;and&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#slang" title="Appendix:Glossary">slang</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To give or deliver (a blow, to someone); to strike or hit.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>took</b> me a blow on the head.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#archaic" title="Appendix:Glossary">archaic</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/visit" title="visit">visit</a>; to include in a course of travel.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1677</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Penn" class="extiw" title="w:William Penn">William Penn</a>, <cite>A Collection of the Works of William Penn:<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="To Which Is Prefixed a Journal of His Life, With Many Original Letters and Papers Not Before Published">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, volume I, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed and Sold by the Assigns of">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> J. Sowle,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="at the Bible in George-Yard, Lombard-Street">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1726</span>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/collectionofwork01penn/page/60/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">60</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Now about a Year ſince, <i>R. B.</i> and <i>B. F.</i> <b>took</b> that City in the Way from <i>Frederickſtadt</i> to <i>Amſterdam</i>, and gave them a Viſit&#58; In which they informed them ſomewhat of <i>Friend's Principles</i>, and recommended the Teſtimony of TRUTH to them, as both a nearer and more certain Thing than the utmoſt of <i>De Labadie</i><span>'</span>s Doctrine. They left them tender and loving.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1793</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Whitehead_(physician)" class="extiw" title="w:John Whitehead (physician)">John Whitehead</a>, <cite>The Life of the Rev. John Wesley, M.A.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span>…<span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, Dublin&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed and solid by">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> John Jones,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="90, Bride-Street">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1805</span>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/liferevjohnwesl00whitgoog/page/n439/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">441</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">But it seems that he did not attend to this circumstance at present&#59; for in May, he set out again for <i>Epworth</i>, and <b>took</b> <i>Manchester</i> in his way, to see his friend Mr. <i>Clayton</i>, who had now left <i>Oxford</i>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#rare" title="Appendix:Glossary">rare</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To portray in a <a href="/wiki/painting" title="painting">painting</a>.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#a." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="ante">a.</abbr></a></i> <b>1701</b> (date written), John Dryden, “To The Pious Memory of the Accomplish'd Young Lady Mrs. <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anne_Killigrew" class="extiw" title="w:Anne Killigrew">Anne Killigrew</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Excellent in the Two Sister-Arts of Poesy and Painting">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>”, in <cite>The Miscellaneous Works of John Dryden,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Esq.; Containing All His Original Poems, Tales, and Translations. Now First Collected and Published Together in Four Volumes. With Explanatory Notes and Observations. Also an Account of His Life and Writings">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, volume II, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed for">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jacob_Tonson" class="extiw" title="w:Jacob Tonson">J<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>acob<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> and R<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>ichard<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Tonson</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="in the Strand">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1760</span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/863244003">→OCLC</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://books.google.com/books?id=EfhdAAAAcAAJ&amp;pg=PA216">page <span class="None" lang="und">216</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Beauty alone could beauty <b>take</b> ſo right&#58; &#47; Her dreſs, her ſhape, her matchleſs grace, &#47; Were all obferv'd, as well as heavenly face.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="use-with-mention">Used in phrasal verbs: <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/take_in#English" title="take in">take in</a></i>&#44;&#32;<i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/take_off#English" title="take off">take off</a></i>&#44;&#32;<i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/take_on#English" title="take on">take on</a></i>&#44;&#32;<i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/take_out#English" title="take out">take out</a></i>&#44;&#32;<i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/take_to#English" title="take to">take to</a></i>&#44;&#32;<i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/take_something_to#English" title="take something to">take something to</a></i>&#44;&#32;<i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/take_up#English" title="take up">take up</a></i>.</span></li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Usage_notes">Usage notes</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Usage notes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li>In a few informal <a href="/wiki/sociolect" title="sociolect">sociolects</a>, the past form <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/took#English" title="took">took</a></i> is sometimes replaced by the <a href="/wiki/proscribed" title="proscribed">proscribed</a> form <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/taked#English" title="taked">taked</a></i>.</li>
<li>Similarly, the participle <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/taken#English" title="taken">taken</a></i> is sometimes replaced by the equally <a href="/wiki/proscribed" title="proscribed">proscribed</a> <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/tooken#English" title="tooken">tooken</a></i>.</li>
<li>In older forms of English, when the pronoun <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/thou#English" title="thou">thou</a></i> was in active use, and verbs used <i class="Latn mention nowrap" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/-est#English" title="-est">-est</a></i> for distinct second-person singular indicative forms, the verb <i>take</i> had the form <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/takest#English" title="takest">takest</a></i>, and had <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/tookest#English" title="tookest">tookest</a></i> for its past tense.</li>
<li>Similarly, when the ending <i class="Latn mention nowrap" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/-eth#English" title="-eth">-eth</a></i> was in active use for third-person singular present indicative forms, the form <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/taketh#English" title="taketh">taketh</a></i> was used.</li></ul>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="en">take</strong> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en p-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/takes#English" title="takes">takes</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li>The or an act of taking.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1999</b></span>, <cite>Report to Congress: Impacts of California Sea Lions and Pacific Harbor Seals on Salmonids and West Coast Ecosystems</cite>, U.S. Department of Commerce, National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration, National Marine Fisheries Service, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://books.google.ca/books?id=_RwXAQAAIAAJ&amp;pg=PA32">page <span class="None" lang="und">32</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">The 1994 Amendments address the incidental <b>take</b> of marine mammals in the course of commercial fishing, not the direct lethal <b>take</b> of pinnipeds for management purposes.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2009</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lissa_Evans" class="extiw" title="w:Lissa Evans">Lissa Evans</a>, <cite>Their Finest Hour and a Half</cite>, London<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span>…<span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Doubleday_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:Doubleday (publisher)">Doubleday</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-385-61423-8" title="Special:BookSources/978-0-385-61423-8">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/theirfinesthoura0000evan/page/321/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">321</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">'I saw you in Norfolk doing twenty-odd <b>takes</b> with that fisherman chap and it looked perfect in the rushes.'</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li>Something that is taken; a <a href="/wiki/haul" title="haul">haul</a>.
<ol><li>Money that is taken in, (legal or illegal) proceeds, income; <span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">in particular</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> profits; takings.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>2018</b> November 26, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Krugman" class="extiw" title="w:Paul Krugman">Paul Krugman</a>, “The Depravity of Climate-Change Denial”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_New_York_Times" class="extiw" title="w:The New York Times">The New York Times</a></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://web.archive.org/web/20230517193607/https://www.nytimes.com/2018/11/26/opinion/climate-change-denial-republican.html">[8]</a></sup>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_New_York_Times_Company" class="extiw" title="w:The New York Times Company">The New York Times Company</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://www.worldcat.org/issn/1553-8095">→ISSN</a></small>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/971436363">→OCLC</a></small>, archived from <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://www.nytimes.com/2018/11/26/opinion/climate-change-denial-republican.html">the original</a> on <span class="mw-formatted-date" title="2023-05-17">17 May 2023</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Why would anyone go along with such things&#63; Money is still the main answer&#58; Almost all prominent climate deniers are on the fossil-fuel <b>take</b>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul>
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He wants half of the <b>take</b> if he helps with the job.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">The mayor is on the <b>take</b>.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li>The or a quantity of <a href="/wiki/fish" title="fish">fish</a>, <a href="/wiki/game" title="game">game</a> animals or <a href="/wiki/pelt" title="pelt">pelts</a>, etc which have been taken at one time; <a href="/wiki/catch" title="catch">catch</a>.</li></ol></li>
<li>An <a href="/wiki/interpretation" title="interpretation">interpretation</a> or <a href="/wiki/view" title="view">view</a>, <a href="/wiki/opinion" title="opinion">opinion</a> or <a href="/wiki/assessment" title="assessment">assessment</a>; <a href="/wiki/perspective" title="perspective">perspective</a>; a <a href="/wiki/statement" title="statement">statement</a> expressing such a <a href="/wiki/position" title="position">position</a>.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/conclusion#English" title="conclusion">conclusion</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/mind#English" title="mind">mind</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/verdict#English" title="verdict">verdict</a></span>; <i>see also</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:judgement#English" title="Thesaurus:judgement">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">judgement</span></a></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">What's your <b>take</b> on this issue, Fred&#63;</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Another unsolicited maths <b>take</b>&#58; talking about quotients in terms of "equivalence classes" or cosets is really unnatural.</i></div></dd></dl>

<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>2018</b> May 10, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ben_Mathis-Lilley" class="extiw" title="w:Ben Mathis-Lilley">Ben Mathis-Lilley</a>, “Fox News Military Analyst Says John McCain Broke Under Torture and Gave Secrets to North Vietnamese”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Slate_(magazine)" class="extiw" title="w:Slate (magazine)">Slate</a></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://web.archive.org/web/20221128221827/https://slate.com/news-and-politics/2018/05/fox-news-analyst-john-mccain-broke-under-torture.html">[10]</a></sup>, archived from <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://slate.com/news-and-politics/2018/05/fox-news-analyst-john-mccain-broke-under-torture.html">the original</a> on <span class="mw-formatted-date" title="2022-11-28">28 November 2022</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I wrote Thursday morning that the Washington Post had printed a column that qualified as the worst <b>take</b> on the debate over whether Gina Haspel, who supported the torture of "War on Terror" detainees, should become CIA director. Putnam's Sons</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/0-399-13616-9" title="Special:BookSources/0-399-13616-9">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/teklords0000shat/page/48/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">48</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">"When our client mentioned Dr. Chesterton, you did a <b>take</b> that was perceptible to one with my trained eye. <li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2007</b></span>, Laura McBride, <cite>Catch a Falling Starr: A Novel</cite>, New York, N.Y.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span>…<span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/iUniverse,_Inc." class="extiw" title="w:iUniverse, Inc.">iUniverse, Inc.</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-595-44510-3" title="Special:BookSources/978-0-595-44510-3">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/catchfallingstar0000mcbr/page/138/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">138</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Biddy did a <span>'</span><b>take</b><span>'</span> and stared at Mandy speechless for a moment—then she fled back to the kitchen.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2013</b></span>, Carsten Stroud, <cite>The Homecoming</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alfred_A._Knopf" class="extiw" title="w:Alfred A. Knopf">Alfred A. Knopf</a>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-307-70096-4" title="Special:BookSources/978-0-307-70096-4">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/homecoming0000stro_s7o3/page/301/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">301</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">He's a stone-cold snake, Nick, but he's our stone—cold snake. Keep tugging on hanging threads and one day your pants will fall off." ¶ Nick did a <b>take</b>, grinning in spite of his miserable mood. "How, exactly, would that work&#63;" ¶ Mavis shrugged, grinned right back at him.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/medicine" title="medicine">medicine</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> An instance of <a href="/wiki/successful" title="successful">successful</a> <a href="/wiki/inoculation" title="inoculation">inoculation</a>/<a href="/wiki/vaccination" title="vaccination">vaccination</a>.</li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/rugby" title="rugby">rugby</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/cricket" title="cricket">cricket</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> A <a href="/wiki/catch" title="catch">catch</a> of the ball (in cricket, especially one by the <a href="/wiki/wicket-keeper" class="mw-redirect" title="wicket-keeper">wicket-keeper</a>).</li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/printing#Noun" title="printing">printing</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> The quantity of <a href="/wiki/copy" title="copy">copy</a> given to a <a href="/wiki/compositor" title="compositor">compositor</a> at one time.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1884</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Southward" class="extiw" title="w:John Southward">John Southward</a>, chapter XXI, in <cite>Practical Printing: A Handbook of the Art of Typography</cite>, second edition, London&#58; J. M. Powell &amp; Son, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/practicalprinti00powegoog/page/197/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">197</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">When the copy arrives, it is taken in hand by the printer, who first of all divides it into "<b>takes</b>" or short portions, distributing these among the various compositors. A <b>take</b> usually consists of a little more than a stickful of matter, but it varies sometimes, for if a new paragraph occurs it is not overlooked. These <b>takes</b> are carefully numbered, and a list is kept of the compositors who take the several pieces.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Verb_2">Verb</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="zh">take</strong></span> <span class="usage-label-term"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hong_Kong_Cantonese" class="extiw" title="w:Hong Kong Cantonese">Hong Kong Cantonese</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span>

<ol><li>to <a href="/wiki/consume" title="consume">consume</a> (<a href="/wiki/drug" title="drug">drugs</a>)
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2012</b></span>, <span class="Hant" lang="zh"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/zh:%E6%94%BF%E5%BA%9C%E6%96%B0%E8%81%9E%E8%99%95" class="extiw" title="w:zh:政府新聞處">政府新聞處</a></span> &#91;<span class="e-translation"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Information_Services_Department" class="extiw" title="w:Information Services Department">Information Services Department</a></span>&#93;, <cite><span class="Hani" lang="zh">企硬 2012（青少年篇）</span></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DZvEs8ePnLE">[14]</a></sup>:</span></div>
<dl><dd><dl class="zhusex"><span lang="zh-Hant" class="Hant"><a href="/wiki/%E4%BC%81%E7%A1%AC#Chinese" title="企硬">企硬</a>！<a href="/w/index.php?title=take%E5%98%A2&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="take嘢 (page does not exist)"><b>take</b>嘢</a><a href="/wiki/%E8%A1%B0#Chinese" title="衰">衰</a><a href="/wiki/%E7%A1%AC#Chinese" title="硬">硬</a>！</span> <span style="color:var(--wikt-palette-forestgreen, #235923); font-size:80%;">&#91;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cantonese" class="extiw" title="w:Cantonese">Cantonese</a>, <i><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Traditional_Chinese" class="extiw" title="w:Traditional Chinese">trad.</a></i> and <i><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Simplified_Chinese" class="extiw" title="w:Simplified Chinese">simp.</a></i>&#93;</span><dd><span lang="zh-Latn" style="color:var(--wikt-palette-darkgrey, #54595d)"><i>kei<sup>5</sup> ngaang<sup>6</sup>! <b>tik<sup>1</sup></b> je<sup>5</sup> seoi<sup>1</sup> ngaang<sup>6</sup>!</i></span> <span style="color:var(--wikt-palette-forestgreen, #235923); font-size:80%;">&#91;Jyutping&#93;</span></dd><dd>Stand firm! <b>Taking</b> drugs certainly causes one to make grave mistakes!</dd></dl></dd></dl></li></ul></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_2">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=take&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

</p>
